The order of the additions is identical to the scalar\n// version, so the results are bit-for-bit the same.\ninline void stencilRow(Real* __restrict const o,\n                       const Real* __restrict const c,\n                       const Real* __restrict const ym, const Real* __restrict const yp,\n                       const Real* __restrict const zm, const Real* __restrict const zp,\n                       const size_t nx) {\n    const size_t last = nx - 1;\n    size_t x = 1;\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n    // Scalar prologue until the store address is 32-byte aligned.\n    while (x < last && (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(o + x) & 31u) != 0) {\n        o[x] = (c[x] + c[x-1] + c[x+1] + ym[x] + yp[x] + zm[x] + zp[x]) / 7.0;\n        ++x;\n    }\n    const __m256d seven = _mm256_set1_pd(7.0);\n    for (; Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) == 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;  // default-init: no zeroing\n        } else {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\nusing Grid = std::vector<Real, DefaultInitAllocator<Real>>;\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    // Parallel over z-slabs: this is also the first touch of the pages, which\n    // pins them to the NUMA node of the thread that will work on them later.\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        Real* __restrict const plane = grid.data() + z * nxy;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < nxy; ++i) {\n            const size_t idx = z * nxy + i;\n            plane[i] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// First touch of the second buffer with the same thread/page distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        Real* __restrict const plane = grid.data() + z * nxy;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < nxy; ++i) {\n            plane[i] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// One row of interior points in x. The result is streamed out with non-temporal\n// stores where available: the output row is never read again in this iteration,\n// so bypassing the caches avoids the write-allocate traffic on this\n// bandwidth-bound kernel. The order of the additions is identical to the scalar\n// version, so the results are bit-for-bit the same.\ninline void stencilRow(Real* __restrict const o,\n                       const Real* __restrict const c,\n                       const Real* __restrict const ym, const Real* __restrict const yp,\n                       const Real* __restrict const zm, const Real* __restrict const zp,\n                       const size_t nx) {\n    const size_t last = nx - 1;\n    size_t x = 1;\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n    // Scalar prologue until the store address is 32-byte aligned.\n    while (x < last && (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(o + x) & 31u) != 0) {\n        o[x] = (c[x] + c[x-1] + c[x+1] + ym[x] + yp[x] + zm[x] + zp[x]) / 7.0;\n        ++x;\n    }\n    const __m256d seven = _mm256_set1_pd(7.0);\n    for (; x + 4 <= last; x += 4) {\n        __m256d v = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x);\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x - 1));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x + 1));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(ym + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(yp + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(zm + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(zp + x));\n        _mm256_stream_pd(o + x, _mm256_div_pd(v, seven));\n    }\n#endif\n#pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t xi = x; xi < last; ++xi) {\n        // Simple averaging stencil\n        o[xi] = (c[xi] + c[xi-1] + c[xi+1] + ym[xi] + yp[xi] + zm[xi] + zp[xi]) / 7.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation\nvoid stencilIteration(const Grid& input,\n                      Grid& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior at all: every point is a boundary point.\n    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) {\n        const size_t total = nxy * nz;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < total; ++i) {\n            out[i] = in[i];\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Interior points, one row of x at a time; the two x-boundary points of\n        // each interior row are copied here as well to keep the row local.\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const c = in + base;\n                const Real* __restrict const ym = c - nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const yp = c + nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const zm = c - nxy;\n                const Real* __restrict const zp = c + nxy;\n                Real* __restrict const o = out + base;\n\n                stencilRow(o, c, ym, yp, zm, zp, nx);\n\n                o[0] = c[0];\n                o[nx-1] = c[nx-1];\n            }\n        }\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n        // Make the non-temporal stores visible to the other threads before the\n        // barriers below.\n        _mm_sfence();\n#endif\n\n        // y-boundary rows of the interior z-planes\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            const size_t base = z * nxy;\n            memcpy(out + base, in + base, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            memcpy(out + base + (ny-1) * nx, in + base + (ny-1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n\n        // The two z-boundary planes\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            memcpy(out + y * nx, in + y * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            const size_t top = (nz-1) * nxy + y * nx;\n            memcpy(out + top, in + top, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Grid& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    const Real* __restrict const data = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(||: bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(data[i]) || std::isinf(data[i])) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min: minVal) reduction(max: maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n    \n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    Grid grid1(gridSize);\n    Grid grid2(gridSize);\n\n    // Initialize (in parallel, which also establishes NUMA page placement)\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    touchGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n    \n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n        } else {\n            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    const Grid& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(std::vector<Real>(finalGrid.begin(), finalGrid.end()), \"Grid\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":46,"oldLines":28,"newStart":46,"newLines":24,"lines":[" }"," "," void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {","-    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;","-    // Parallel over z-slabs: this is also the first touch of the pages, which","-    // pins them to the NUMA node of the thread that will work on them later.","+    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();","+    const size_t total = nx * ny * nz;","+    // Flat parallel loop: this is also the first touch of the pages, so they end","+    // up on the NUMA node of the thread that works on that part of the grid","+    // during the stencil iterations."," #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)","-    for (size_t z = 0; A purely\n// one-dimensional split would give each thread a very thin slab at high thread\n// counts, and the two halo planes it has to read would then dominate its memory\n// traffic. Pick the factorization of the team size that minimizes that halo\n// overhead for the given grid shape.\nsize_t chooseZParts(const size_t nthreads, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    size_t bestPz = 1;\n    double bestCost = 1e300;\n    for (size_t pz = 1; pz <= nthreads; ++pz) {\n        if (nthreads % pz != 0) continue;\n        const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n        const double slab = (double)nz / (double)pz;\n        const double band = (double)ny / (double)py;\n        // Halo traffic overhead, times the penalty for threads that would fall\n        // outside the grid and stay idle.\n        double cost = (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(slab, 1.0)) * (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(band, 1.0));\n        if (slab < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / slab;\n        if (band < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / band;\n        if (cost < bestCost) {\n            bestCost = cost;\n            bestPz = pz;\n        }\n    }\n    return bestPz;\n}\n\n// Tile of the (z, y) plane owned by the calling thread.\nstruct Tile {\n    size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n};\n\ninline Tile myTile(const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    const size_t nthreads = (size_t)omp_get_num_threads();\n    const size_t tid = (size_t)omp_get_thread_num();\n    const size_t pz = chooseZParts(nthreads, ny, nz);\n    const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n    Tile t;\n    splitRange(nz, pz, tid / py, t.z0, t.z1);\n    splitRange(ny, py, tid % py, t.y0, t.y1);\n    return t;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    // Each thread initializes exactly the rows it will own during the stencil\n    // iterations, so this first touch places the pages on its NUMA node.\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n        for (size_t z = t.z0; pz <= nthreads; ++pz) {\n        if (nthreads % pz != 0) continue;\n        const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n        const double slab = (double)nz / (double)pz;\n        const double band = (double)ny / (double)py;\n        // Halo traffic overhead, times the penalty for threads that would fall\n        // outside the grid and stay idle.\n        double cost = (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(slab, 1.0)) * (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(band, 1.0));\n        if (slab < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / slab;\n        if (band < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / band;\n        if (cost < bestCost) {\n            bestCost = cost;\n            bestPz = pz;\n        }\n    }\n    return bestPz;\n}\n\n// Tile of the (z, y) plane owned by the calling thread.\nstruct Tile {\n    size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n};\n\ninline Tile myTile(const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    const size_t nthreads = (size_t)omp_get_num_threads();\n    const size_t tid = (size_t)omp_get_thread_num();\n    const size_t pz = chooseZParts(nthreads, ny, nz);\n    const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n    Tile t;\n    splitRange(nz, pz, tid / py, t.z0, t.z1);\n    splitRange(ny, py, tid % py, t.y0, t.y1);\n    return t;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    // Each thread initializes exactly the rows it will own during the stencil\n    // iterations, so this first touch places the pages on its NUMA node.\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n        for (size_t z = t.z0; z < t.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = t.y0; y < t.y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    g[base + x] = ((base + x) % 19) * 1.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// First touch of the second buffer with the same page distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n        for (size_t z = t.z0; z < t.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = t.y0; y < t.y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n                memset(g + base, 0, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <memory>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n#include <immintrin.h>\n#endif\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n// Allocator that leaves memory uninitialized on resize, so that the grids can be\n// first-touched by the OpenMP threads that later own them (NUMA page placement).\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct DefaultInitAllocator : std::allocator<T> {\n    using std::allocator<T>::allocator;\n\n    template <typename U>\n    struct rebind {\n        using other = DefaultInitAllocator<U>;\n    };\n\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) == 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;  // default-init: no zeroing\n        } else {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\nusing Grid = std::vector<Real, DefaultInitAllocator<Real>>;\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t total = nx * ny * nz;\n    // Flat parallel loop: this is also the first touch of the pages, so they end\n    // up on the NUMA node of the thread that works on that part of the grid\n    // during the stencil iterations.\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < total; ++idx) {\n        g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// First touch of the second buffer with the same page distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t total = nx * ny * nz;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < total; ++idx) {\n        g[idx] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// One row of interior points in x. The result is streamed out with non-temporal\n// stores where available: the output row is never read again in this iteration,\n// so bypassing the caches avoids the write-allocate traffic on this\n// bandwidth-bound kernel. The order of the additions is identical to the scalar\n// version, so the results are bit-for-bit the same.\ninline void stencilRow(Real* __restrict const o,\n                       const Real* __restrict const c,\n                       const Real* __restrict const ym, const Real* __restrict const yp,\n                       const Real* __restrict const zm, const Real* __restrict const zp,\n                       const size_t nx) {\n    const size_t last = nx - 1;\n    size_t x = 1;\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n    // Scalar prologue until the store address is 32-byte aligned.\n    while (x < last && (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(o + x) & 31u) != 0) {\n        o[x] = (c[x] + c[x-1] + c[x+1] + ym[x] + yp[x] + zm[x] + zp[x]) / 7.0;\n        ++x;\n    }\n    const __m256d seven = _mm256_set1_pd(7.0);\n    for (; x + 4 <= last; x += 4) {\n        __m256d v = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x);\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x - 1));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x + 1));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(ym + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(yp + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(zm + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(zp + x));\n        _mm256_stream_pd(o + x, _mm256_div_pd(v, seven));\n    }\n#endif\n#pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t xi = x; xi < last; ++xi) {\n        // Simple averaging stencil\n        o[xi] = (c[xi] + c[xi-1] + c[xi+1] + ym[xi] + yp[xi] + zm[xi] + zp[xi]) / 7.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation\nvoid stencilIteration(const Grid& input,\n                      Grid& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior at all: every point is a boundary point.\n    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) {\n        const size_t total = nxy * nz;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < total; ++i) {\n            out[i] = in[i];\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Interior points, one row of x at a time; the two x-boundary points of\n        // each interior row are copied here as well to keep the row local.\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const c = in + base;\n                const Real* __restrict const ym = c - nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const yp = c + nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const zm = c - nxy;\n                const Real* __restrict const zp = c + nxy;\n                Real* __restrict const o = out + base;\n\n                stencilRow(o, c, ym, yp, zm, zp, nx);\n\n                o[0] = c[0];\n                o[nx-1] = c[nx-1];\n            }\n        }\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n        // Make the non-temporal stores visible to the other threads before the\n        // barriers below.\n        _mm_sfence();\n#endif\n\n        // y-boundary rows of the interior z-planes\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            const size_t base = z * nxy;\n            memcpy(out + base, in + base, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            memcpy(out + base + (ny-1) * nx, in + base + (ny-1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n\n        // The two z-boundary planes\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            memcpy(out + y * nx, in + y * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            const size_t top = (nz-1) * nxy + y * nx;\n            memcpy(out + top, in + top, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Grid& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    const Real* __restrict const data = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(||: bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(data[i]) || std::isinf(data[i])) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min: minVal) reduction(max: maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) == 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;  // default-init: no zeroing\n        } else {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\nusing Grid = std::vector<Real, DefaultInitAllocator<Real>>;\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Split [0, n) into `parts` nearly equal pieces and return piece number `idx`.\ninline void splitRange(const size_t n, const size_t parts, const size_t idx,\n                       size_t& begin, size_t& end) noexcept {\n    const size_t q = n / parts;\n    const size_t r = n % parts;\n    begin = idx * q + (idx < r ? idx : r);\n    end = begin + q + (idx < r ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// The threads are arranged as a pz x py grid of (z-slab, y-band) tiles. A purely\n// one-dimensional split would give each thread a very thin slab at high thread\n// counts, and the two halo planes it has to read would then dominate its memory\n// traffic. Pick the factorization of the team size that minimizes that halo\n// overhead for the given grid shape.\nsize_t chooseZParts(const size_t nthreads, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    size_t bestPz = 1;\n    double bestCost = 1e300;\n    for (size_t pz = 1; pz <= nthreads; ++pz) {\n        if (nthreads % pz != 0) continue;\n        const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n        const double slab = (double)nz / (double)pz;\n        const double band = (double)ny / (double)py;\n        // Halo traffic overhead, times the penalty for threads that would fall\n        // outside the grid and stay idle.\n        double cost = (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(slab, 1.0)) * (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(band, 1.0));\n        if (slab < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / slab;\n        if (band < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / band;\n        if (cost < bestCost) {\n            bestCost = cost;\n            bestPz = pz;\n        }\n    }\n    return bestPz;\n}\n\n// Tile of the (z, y) plane owned by the calling thread.\nstruct Tile {\n    size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n};\n\ninline Tile myTile(const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    const size_t nthreads = (size_t)omp_get_num_threads();\n    const size_t tid = (size_t)omp_get_thread_num();\n    const size_t pz = chooseZParts(nthreads, ny, nz);\n    const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n    Tile t;\n    splitRange(nz, pz, tid / py, t.z0, t.z1);\n    splitRange(ny, py, tid % py, t.y0, t.y1);\n    return t;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    // Each thread initializes exactly the rows it will own during the stencil\n    // iterations, so this first touch places the pages on its NUMA node.\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n        for (size_t z = t.z0; z < t.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = t.y0; y < t.y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    g[base + x] = ((base + x) % 19) * 1.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// First touch of the second buffer with the same page distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n        for (size_t z = t.z0; z < t.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = t.y0; y < t.y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n                memset(g + base, 0, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// One row of interior points in x. The result is streamed out with non-temporal\n// stores where available: the output row is never read again in this iteration,\n// so bypassing the caches avoids the write-allocate traffic on this\n// bandwidth-bound kernel. The order of the additions is identical to the scalar\n// version, so the results are bit-for-bit the same.\ninline void stencilRow(Real* __restrict const o,\n                       const Real* __restrict const c,\n                       const Real* __restrict const ym, const Real* __restrict const yp,\n                       const Real* __restrict const zm, const Real* __restrict const zp,\n                       const size_t nx) {\n    const size_t last = nx - 1;\n    size_t x = 1;\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n    // Scalar prologue until the store address is 32-byte aligned.\n    while (x < last && (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(o + x) & 31u) != 0) {\n        o[x] = (c[x] + c[x-1] + c[x+1] + ym[x] + yp[x] + zm[x] + zp[x]) / 7.0;\n        ++x;\n    }\n    const __m256d seven = _mm256_set1_pd(7.0);\n    for (; x + 4 <= last; x += 4) {\n        __m256d v = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x);\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x - 1));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x + 1));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(ym + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(yp + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(zm + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(zp + x));\n        _mm256_stream_pd(o + x, _mm256_div_pd(v, seven));\n    }\n#endif\n#pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t xi = x; xi < last; ++xi) {\n        // Simple averaging stencil\n        o[xi] = (c[xi] + c[xi-1] + c[xi+1] + ym[xi] + yp[xi] + zm[xi] + zp[xi]) / 7.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation\nvoid stencilIteration(const Grid& input,\n                      Grid& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior at all: every point is a boundary point.\n    if (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3) {\n        const size_t total = nxy * nz;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < total; ++i) {\n            out[i] = in[i];\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Interior points, one row of x at a time; the two x-boundary points of\n        // each interior row are copied here as well to keep the row local.\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const c = in + base;\n                const Real* __restrict const ym = c - nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const yp = c + nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const zm = c - nxy;\n                const Real* __restrict const zp = c + nxy;\n                Real* __restrict const o = out + base;\n\n                stencilRow(o, c, ym, yp, zm, zp, nx);\n\n                o[0] = c[0];\n                o[nx-1] = c[nx-1];\n            }\n        }\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n        // Make the non-temporal stores visible to the other threads before the\n        // barriers below.\n        _mm_sfence();\n#endif\n\n        // y-boundary rows of the interior z-planes\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            const size_t base = z * nxy;\n            memcpy(out + base, in + base, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            memcpy(out + base + (ny-1) * nx, in + base + (ny-1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n\n        // The two z-boundary planes\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            memcpy(out + y * nx, in + y * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            const size_t top = (nz-1) * nxy + y * nx;\n            memcpy(out + top, in + top, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Grid& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    const Real* __restrict const data = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(||: bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(data[i]) || std::isinf(data[i])) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min: minVal) reduction(max: maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n    \n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    Grid grid1(gridSize);\n    Grid grid2(gridSize);\n\n    // Initialize (in parallel, which also establishes NUMA page placement)\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    touchGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n    \n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) == 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;  // default-init: no zeroing\n        } else {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\nusing Grid = std::vector<Real, DefaultInitAllocator<Real>>;\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Split [0, n) into `parts` nearly equal pieces and return piece number `idx`.\ninline void splitRange(const size_t n, const size_t parts, const size_t idx,\n                       size_t& begin, size_t& end) noexcept {\n    const size_t q = n / parts;\n    const size_t r = n % parts;\n    begin = idx * q + (idx < r ? idx : r);\n    end = begin + q + (idx < r ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// The threads are arranged as a pz x py grid of (z-slab, y-band) tiles. A purely\n// one-dimensional split would give each thread a very thin slab at high thread\n// counts, and the two halo planes it has to read would then dominate its memory\n// traffic. Pick the factorization of the team size that minimizes that halo\n// overhead for the given grid shape.\nsize_t chooseZParts(const size_t nthreads, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    size_t bestPz = 1;\n    double bestCost = 1e300;\n    for (size_t pz = 1; pz <= nthreads; ++pz) {\n        if (nthreads % pz != 0) continue;\n        const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n        const double slab = (double)nz / (double)pz;\n        const double band = (double)ny / (double)py;\n        // Halo traffic overhead, times the penalty for threads that would fall\n        // outside the grid and stay idle.\n        double cost = (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(slab, 1.0)) * (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(band, 1.0));\n        if (slab < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / slab;\n        if (band < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / band;\n        if (cost < bestCost) {\n            bestCost = cost;\n            bestPz = pz;\n        }\n    }\n    return bestPz;\n}\n\n// Tile of the (z, y) plane owned by the calling thread.\nstruct Tile {\n    size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n};\n\ninline Tile myTile(const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    const size_t nthreads = (size_t)omp_get_num_threads();\n    const size_t tid = (size_t)omp_get_thread_num();\n    const size_t pz = chooseZParts(nthreads, ny, nz);\n    const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n    Tile t;\n    splitRange(nz, pz, tid / py, t.z0, t.z1);\n    splitRange(ny, py, tid % py, t.y0, t.y1);\n    return t;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    // Each thread initializes exactly the rows it will own during the stencil\n    // iterations, so this first touch places the pages on its NUMA node.\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n        for (size_t z = t.z0; The result is streamed out with non-temporal\n// stores where available: the output row is never read again in this iteration,\n// so bypassing the caches avoids the write-allocate traffic on this\n// bandwidth-bound kernel. The order of the additions is identical to the scalar\n// version, so the results are bit-for-bit the same.\ninline void stencilRow(Real* __restrict const o,\n                       const Real* __restrict const c,\n                       const Real* __restrict const ym, const Real* __restrict const yp,\n                       const Real* __restrict const zm, const Real* __restrict const zp,\n                       const size_t nx) {\n    const size_t last = nx - 1;\n    size_t x = 1;\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n    // Scalar prologue until the store address is 32-byte aligned.\n    while (x < last && (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(o + x) & 31u) != 0) {\n        o[x] = (c[x] + c[x-1] + c[x+1] + ym[x] + yp[x] + zm[x] + zp[x]) / 7.0;\n        ++x;\n    }\n    const __m256d seven = _mm256_set1_pd(7.0);\n    for (; Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) == 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;  // default-init: no zeroing\n        } else {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\nusing Grid = std::vector<Real, DefaultInitAllocator<Real>>;\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Split [0, n) into `parts` nearly equal pieces and return piece number `idx`.\ninline void splitRange(const size_t n, const size_t parts, const size_t idx,\n                       size_t& begin, size_t& end) noexcept {\n    const size_t q = n / parts;\n    const size_t r = n % parts;\n    begin = idx * q + (idx < r ? idx : r);\n    end = begin + q + (idx < r ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// The threads are arranged as a pz x py grid of (z-slab, y-band) tiles. A purely\n// one-dimensional split would give each thread a very thin slab at high thread\n// counts, and the two halo planes it has to read would then dominate its memory\n// traffic. Pick the factorization of the team size that minimizes that halo\n// overhead for the given grid shape.\nsize_t chooseZParts(const size_t nthreads, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    size_t bestPz = 1;\n    double bestCost = 1e300;\n    for (size_t pz = 1; pz <= nthreads; ++pz) {\n        if (nthreads % pz != 0) continue;\n        const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n        const double slab = (double)nz / (double)pz;\n        const double band = (double)ny / (double)py;\n        // Halo traffic overhead, times the penalty for threads that would fall\n        // outside the grid and stay idle.\n        double cost = (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(slab, 1.0)) * (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(band, 1.0));\n        if (slab < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / slab;\n        if (band < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / band;\n        if (cost < bestCost) {\n            bestCost = cost;\n            bestPz = pz;\n        }\n    }\n    return bestPz;\n}\n\n// Tile of the (z, y) plane owned by the calling thread.\nstruct Tile {\n    size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n};\n\ninline Tile myTile(const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    const size_t nthreads = (size_t)omp_get_num_threads();\n    const size_t tid = (size_t)omp_get_thread_num();\n    const size_t pz = chooseZParts(nthreads, ny, nz);\n    const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n    Tile t;\n    splitRange(nz, pz, tid / py, t.z0, t.z1);\n    splitRange(ny, py, tid % py, t.y0, t.y1);\n    return t;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    // Each thread initializes exactly the rows it will own during the stencil\n    // iterations, so this first touch places the pages on its NUMA node.\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n        for (size_t z = t.z0; z < t.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = t.y0; y < t.y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    g[base + x] = ((base + x) % 19) * 1.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// First touch of the second buffer with the same page distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n        for (size_t z = t.z0; z < t.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = t.y0; y < t.y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n                memset(g + base, 0, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// One row of interior points in x. The result is streamed out with non-temporal\n// stores where available: the output row is never read again in this iteration,\n// so bypassing the caches avoids the write-allocate traffic on this\n// bandwidth-bound kernel. The order of the additions is identical to the scalar\n// version, so the results are bit-for-bit the same.\ninline void stencilRow(Real* __restrict const o,\n                       const Real* __restrict const c,\n                       const Real* __restrict const ym, const Real* __restrict const yp,\n                       const Real* __restrict const zm, const Real* __restrict const zp,\n                       const size_t nx) {\n    const size_t last = nx - 1;\n    size_t x = 1;\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n    // Scalar prologue until the store address is 32-byte aligned.\n    while (x < last && (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(o + x) & 31u) != 0) {\n        o[x] = (c[x] + c[x-1] + c[x+1] + ym[x] + yp[x] + zm[x] + zp[x]) / 7.0;\n        ++x;\n    }\n    const __m256d seven = _mm256_set1_pd(7.0);\n    for (; x + 4 <= last; x += 4) {\n        __m256d v = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x);\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x - 1));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x + 1));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(ym + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(yp + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(zm + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(zp + x));\n        _mm256_stream_pd(o + x, _mm256_div_pd(v, seven));\n    }\n#endif\n#pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t xi = x; xi < last; ++xi) {\n        // Simple averaging stencil\n        o[xi] = (c[xi] + c[xi-1] + c[xi+1] + ym[xi] + yp[xi] + zm[xi] + zp[xi]) / 7.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation for the rows owned by the calling thread.\ninline void stencilIterationTile(const Real* __restrict const in,\n                                 Real* __restrict const out,\n                                 const Tile& t,\n                                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior at all: every point is a boundary point.\n    const bool allBoundary = (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3);\n\n    for (size_t z = t.z0; z < t.z1; ++z) {\n        const bool zBoundary = allBoundary || z == 0 || z == nz - 1;\n        for (size_t y = t.y0; y < t.y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const Real* __restrict const c = in + base;\n            Real* __restrict const o = out + base;\n\n            if (zBoundary || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                // Copy boundary values\n                memcpy(o, c, nx * sizeof(Real));\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            stencilRow(o, c, c - nx, c + nx, c - nxy, c + nxy, nx);\n\n            // x boundaries of this row\n            o[0] = c[0];\n            o[nx-1] = c[nx-1];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Grid& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    const Real* __restrict const data = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(||: bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(data[i]) || std::isinf(data[i])) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min: minVal) reduction(max: maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) == 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;  // default-init: no zeroing\n        } else {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\nusing Grid = std::vector<Real, DefaultInitAllocator<Real>>;\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Split [0, n) into `parts` nearly equal pieces and return piece number `idx`.\ninline void splitRange(const size_t n, const size_t parts, const size_t idx,\n                       size_t& begin, size_t& end) noexcept {\n    const size_t q = n / parts;\n    const size_t r = n % parts;\n    begin = idx * q + (idx < r ? idx : r);\n    end = begin + q + (idx < r ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// The threads are arranged as a pz x py grid of (z-slab, y-band) tiles. A purely\n// one-dimensional split would give each thread a very thin slab at high thread\n// counts, and the two halo planes it has to read would then dominate its memory\n// traffic. Pick the factorization of the team size that minimizes that halo\n// overhead for the given grid shape.\nsize_t chooseZParts(const size_t nthreads, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    size_t bestPz = 1;\n    double bestCost = 1e300;\n    for (size_t pz = 1; pz <= nthreads; ++pz) {\n        if (nthreads % pz != 0) continue;\n        const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n        const double slab = (double)nz / (double)pz;\n        const double band = (double)ny / (double)py;\n        // Halo traffic overhead, times the penalty for threads that would fall\n        // outside the grid and stay idle.\n        double cost = (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(slab, 1.0)) * (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(band, 1.0));\n        if (slab < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / slab;\n        if (band < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / band;\n        if (cost < bestCost) {\n            bestCost = cost;\n            bestPz = pz;\n        }\n    }\n    return bestPz;\n}\n\n// Tile of the (z, y) plane owned by the calling thread.\nstruct Tile {\n    size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n};\n\ninline Tile myTile(const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    const size_t nthreads = (size_t)omp_get_num_threads();\n    const size_t tid = (size_t)omp_get_thread_num();\n    const size_t pz = chooseZParts(nthreads, ny, nz);\n    const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n    Tile t;\n    splitRange(nz, pz, tid / py, t.z0, t.z1);\n    splitRange(ny, py, tid % py, t.y0, t.y1);\n    return t;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    // Each thread initializes exactly the rows it will own during the stencil\n    // iterations, so this first touch places the pages on its NUMA node.\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n        for (size_t z = t.z0; The result is streamed out with non-temporal\n// stores where available: the output row is never read again in this iteration,\n// so bypassing the caches avoids the write-allocate traffic on this\n// bandwidth-bound kernel. The order of the additions is identical to the scalar\n// version, so the results are bit-for-bit the same.\ninline void stencilRow(Real* __restrict const o,\n                       const Real* __restrict const c,\n                       const Real* __restrict const ym, const Real* __restrict const yp,\n                       const Real* __restrict const zm, const Real* __restrict const zp,\n                       const size_t nx) {\n    const size_t last = nx - 1;\n    size_t x = 1;\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n    // Scalar prologue until the store address is 32-byte aligned.\n    while (x < last && (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(o + x) & 31u) != 0) {\n        o[x] = (c[x] + c[x-1] + c[x+1] + ym[x] + yp[x] + zm[x] + zp[x]) / 7.0;\n        ++x;\n    }\n    const __m256d seven = _mm256_set1_pd(7.0);\n    for (; Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min: minVal) reduction(max: maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; Thread migration would both destroy the NUMA\n// locality established by the first touch and make the per-iteration barrier\n// much more expensive, and OpenMP does not bind threads by default. Any affinity\n// setting made by the user takes precedence: in that case nothing is done here.\nvoid bindThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") || getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\")) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Split the usable CPUs into one per physical core plus the SMT siblings, so\n    // that the siblings are only used once every core carries a thread.\n    std::vector<int> cores;\n    std::vector<int> siblings;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", cpu);\n        int first = cpu;\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            int v = cpu;\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &v) == 1) first = v;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        (first == cpu ? Thread migration would both destroy the NUMA\n// locality established by the first touch and make the per-iteration barrier\n// much more expensive, and OpenMP does not bind threads by default. Any affinity\n// setting made by the user takes precedence: in that case nothing is done here.\nvoid bindThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") || getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\")) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Split the usable CPUs into one per physical core plus the SMT siblings, so\n    // that the siblings are only used once every core carries a thread.\n    std::vector<int> cores;\n    std::vector<int> siblings;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", cpu);\n        int first = cpu;\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            int v = cpu;\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &v) == 1) first = v;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        (first == cpu ? cores : siblings).push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (cores.empty()) return;\n\n    std::vector<int> all = cores;\n    all.insert(all.end(), siblings.begin(), siblings.end());\n    const std::vector<int>& pool =\n        ((size_t)omp_get_max_threads() <= cores.size()) ? cores : all;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t nthreads = (size_t)omp_get_num_threads();\n        const size_t tid = (size_t)omp_get_thread_num();\n        // Spread the threads evenly over the pool.\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(pool[(tid * pool.size()) / nthreads], &set);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n}\n\n// Split [0, n) into `parts` nearly equal pieces and return piece number `idx`.","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <memory>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n#include <immintrin.h>\n#endif\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n// Allocator that leaves memory uninitialized on resize, so that the grids can be\n// first-touched by the OpenMP threads that later own them (NUMA page placement).\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct DefaultInitAllocator : std::allocator<T> {\n    using std::allocator<T>::allocator;\n\n    template <typename U>\n    struct rebind {\n        using other = DefaultInitAllocator<U>;\n    };\n\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) == 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;  // default-init: no zeroing\n        } else {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\nusing Grid = std::vector<Real, DefaultInitAllocator<Real>>;\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Split [0, n) into `parts` nearly equal pieces and return piece number `idx`.\ninline void splitRange(const size_t n, const size_t parts, const size_t idx,\n                       size_t& begin, size_t& end) noexcept {\n    const size_t q = n / parts;\n    const size_t r = n % parts;\n    begin = idx * q + (idx < r ? idx : r);\n    end = begin + q + (idx < r ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// The threads are arranged as a pz x py grid of (z-slab, y-band) tiles. A purely\n// one-dimensional split would give each thread a very thin slab at high thread\n// counts, and the two halo planes it has to read would then dominate its memory\n// traffic. Pick the factorization of the team size that minimizes that halo\n// overhead for the given grid shape.\nsize_t chooseZParts(const size_t nthreads, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    size_t bestPz = 1;\n    double bestCost = 1e300;\n    for (size_t pz = 1; pz <= nthreads; ++pz) {\n        if (nthreads % pz != 0) continue;\n        const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n        const double slab = (double)nz / (double)pz;\n        const double band = (double)ny / (double)py;\n        // Halo traffic overhead, times the penalty for threads that would fall\n        // outside the grid and stay idle.\n        double cost = (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(slab, 1.0)) * (1.0 + 2.0 / std::max(band, 1.0));\n        if (slab < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / slab;\n        if (band < 1.0) cost *= 1.0 / band;\n        if (cost < bestCost) {\n            bestCost = cost;\n            bestPz = pz;\n        }\n    }\n    return bestPz;\n}\n\n// Tile of the (z, y) plane owned by the calling thread.\nstruct Tile {\n    size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n};\n\ninline Tile myTile(const size_t ny, const size_t nz) noexcept {\n    const size_t nthreads = (size_t)omp_get_num_threads();\n    const size_t tid = (size_t)omp_get_thread_num();\n    const size_t pz = chooseZParts(nthreads, ny, nz);\n    const size_t py = nthreads / pz;\n    Tile t;\n    splitRange(nz, pz, tid / py, t.z0, t.z1);\n    splitRange(ny, py, tid % py, t.y0, t.y1);\n    return t;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    // Each thread initializes exactly the rows it will own during the stencil\n    // iterations, so this first touch places the pages on its NUMA node.\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n        for (size_t z = t.z0; z < t.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = t.y0; y < t.y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    g[base + x] = ((base + x) % 19) * 1.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// First touch of the second buffer with the same page distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(Grid& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n        for (size_t z = t.z0; z < t.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = t.y0; y < t.y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n                memset(g + base, 0, nx * sizeof(Real));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// One row of interior points in x. The result is streamed out with non-temporal\n// stores where available: the output row is never read again in this iteration,\n// so bypassing the caches avoids the write-allocate traffic on this\n// bandwidth-bound kernel. The order of the additions is identical to the scalar\n// version, so the results are bit-for-bit the same.\ninline void stencilRow(Real* __restrict const o,\n                       const Real* __restrict const c,\n                       const Real* __restrict const ym, const Real* __restrict const yp,\n                       const Real* __restrict const zm, const Real* __restrict const zp,\n                       const size_t nx) {\n    const size_t last = nx - 1;\n    size_t x = 1;\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n    // Scalar prologue until the store address is 32-byte aligned.\n    while (x < last && (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(o + x) & 31u) != 0) {\n        o[x] = (c[x] + c[x-1] + c[x+1] + ym[x] + yp[x] + zm[x] + zp[x]) / 7.0;\n        ++x;\n    }\n    const __m256d seven = _mm256_set1_pd(7.0);\n    for (; x + 4 <= last; x += 4) {\n        __m256d v = _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x);\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x - 1));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(c + x + 1));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(ym + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(yp + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(zm + x));\n        v = _mm256_add_pd(v, _mm256_loadu_pd(zp + x));\n        _mm256_stream_pd(o + x, _mm256_div_pd(v, seven));\n    }\n#endif\n#pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t xi = x; xi < last; ++xi) {\n        // Simple averaging stencil\n        o[xi] = (c[xi] + c[xi-1] + c[xi+1] + ym[xi] + yp[xi] + zm[xi] + zp[xi]) / 7.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation for the rows owned by the calling thread.\ninline void stencilIterationTile(const Real* __restrict const in,\n                                 Real* __restrict const out,\n                                 const Tile& t,\n                                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    // Degenerate grids have no interior at all: every point is a boundary point.\n    const bool allBoundary = (nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3);\n\n    for (size_t z = t.z0; z < t.z1; ++z) {\n        const bool zBoundary = allBoundary || z == 0 || z == nz - 1;\n        for (size_t y = t.y0; y < t.y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const Real* __restrict const c = in + base;\n            Real* __restrict const o = out + base;\n\n            if (zBoundary || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                // Copy boundary values\n                memcpy(o, c, nx * sizeof(Real));\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            stencilRow(o, c, c - nx, c + nx, c - nxy, c + nxy, nx);\n\n            // x boundaries of this row\n            o[0] = c[0];\n            o[nx-1] = c[nx-1];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Run all iterations inside a single parallel region: the team, the tile\n// assignment and the thread/data affinity are then set up only once, and the\n// only per-iteration synchronization is one barrier.\nvoid runStencil(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                const int iterations) {\n    Real* __restrict const g1 = grid1.data();\n    Real* __restrict const g2 = grid2.data();\n\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        // Every thread owns a fixed tile of rows: the same one it initialized.\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n                stencilIterationTile(g1, g2, t, nx, ny, nz);\n            } else {\n                stencilIterationTile(g2, g1, t, nx, ny, nz);\n            }\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n            // Make the non-temporal stores visible to the threads that read this\n            // tile's halo in the next iteration.\n            _mm_sfence();\n#endif\n#pragma omp barrier\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Grid& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    const Real* __restrict const data = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(||: bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(data[i]) || std::isinf(data[i])) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min: minVal) reduction(max: maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n    \n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    Grid grid1(gridSize);\n    Grid grid2(gridSize);\n\n    // Initialize (in parallel, which also establishes NUMA page placement)\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    touchGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n    \n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    runStencil(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz, iterations);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    const Grid& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(std::vector<Real>(finalGrid.begin(), finalGrid.end()), \"Grid\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":45,"oldLines":6,"newStart":45,"newLines":56,"lines":["     return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;"," }"," ","+// Pin every thread to its own CPU. Thread migration would both destroy the NUMA","+// locality established by the first touch and make the per-iteration barrier","+// much more expensive, and OpenMP does not bind threads by default. Any affinity","+// setting made by the user takes precedence: in that case nothing is done here.","+void bindThreads() {","+    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") || getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\")) {","+        return;","+    }","+","+    cpu_set_t allowed;","+    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);","+    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {","+        return;","+    }","+","+    // Split the usable CPUs into one per physical core plus the SMT siblings, so","+    // that the siblings are only used once every core carries a thread.","+    std::vector<int> cores;","+    std::vector<int> siblings;","+    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {","+        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;","+        char path[128];","+        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", cpu);","+        int first = cpu;","+        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {","+            int v = cpu;","+            if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &v) == 1) first = v;","+            fclose(f);","+        }","+        (first == cpu ? cores : siblings).push_back(cpu);","+    }","+    if (cores.empty()) return;","+","+    std::vector<int> all = cores;","+    all.insert(all.end(), siblings.begin(), siblings.end());","+    const std::vector<int>& pool =","+        ((size_t)omp_get_max_threads() <= cores.size()) ? z < t.z1; ++z) {\n        const bool zBoundary = allBoundary || z == 0 || z == nz - 1;\n        for (size_t y = t.y0; y < t.y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const Real* __restrict const c = in + base;\n            Real* __restrict const o = out + base;\n\n            if (zBoundary || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                // Copy boundary values\n                memcpy(o, c, nx * sizeof(Real));\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            stencilRow(o, c, c - nx, c + nx, c - nxy, c + nxy, nx);\n\n            // x boundaries of this row\n            o[0] = c[0];\n            o[nx-1] = c[nx-1];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Run all iterations inside a single parallel region: the team, the tile\n// assignment and the thread/data affinity are then set up only once, and the\n// only per-iteration synchronization is one barrier.\nvoid runStencil(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                const int iterations) {\n    Real* __restrict const g1 = grid1.data();\n    Real* __restrict const g2 = grid2.data();\n\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        // Every thread owns a fixed tile of rows: the same one it initialized.\n        const Tile t = myTile(ny, nz);\n\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n                stencilIterationTile(g1, g2, t, nx, ny, nz);\n            } else {\n                stencilIterationTile(g2, g1, t, nx, ny, nz);\n            }\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n            // Make the non-temporal stores visible to the threads that read this\n            // tile's halo in the next iteration.\n            _mm_sfence();\n#endif\n#pragma omp barrier\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Grid& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    const Real* __restrict const data = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(||: bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(data[i]) || std::isinf(data[i])) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min: minVal) reduction(max: maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded","numLines":110,"startLine":204,"totalLines":429}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CekEVwL88fEgtsdSzMVbA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp` and `stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt` are parallelized with OpenMP (executable name unchanged, no new files or dependencies — the build dir I used for testing was removed).\n\n**What the parallel version does**\n\n1. **2D (z,y) tile decomposition.** Each thread owns a fixed tile of rows for the whole run; the team size is factored into `pz × py` by picking the factorization that minimizes halo-read overhead for the grid shape. A plain 1D split gives each thread a 2-plane slab at 256 threads, where the two halo planes nearly double its read traffic.\n2. **NUMA first touch.** The grids use an allocator that skips `std::vector`'s zero-fill, so the pages are first touched by the thread that owns them, with exactly the same tiling as the compute loop. Both buffers are touched before the timed region.\n3. **Explicit thread pinning** (`sched_setaffinity`, cores before SMT siblings), skipped when `OMP_PROC_BIND`/`OMP_PLACES`/`GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY` is set. libgomp doesn't bind by default and ignores a `proc_bind` clause then; unpinned runs lost up to 10x on small grids.\n4. **One parallel region for all iterations**, with a single barrier per iteration instead of a fork/join.\n5. **AVX kernel with non-temporal stores** for the interior rows (output isn't re-read that iteration), removing write-allocate traffic; `_mm_sfence()` before each barrier. Guarded by `#if defined(__AVX__)` with a plain `omp simd` fallback.\n6. Boundary copies are fused into each thread's own tile loop (row `memcpy` / two x-endpoints), so they need no extra pass or barrier. Validation loops are parallel reductions.\n\n**Correctness:** the addition order and the `/ 7.0` division are unchanged, so results are bit-identical. Verified against the original serial code — identical output hashes for `-x 64 -i 7`, `-x 65 -y 33 -z 17 -i 4`, `-x 5 -i 3`, `-x 2 -y 40 -z 40 -i 3` (degenerate dim), `-x 4 -y 3 -z 259 -i 5`, `-x 1 -i 2`, `-x 128 -i 10`, each at 1, 3, 100 and 256 threads.\n\n**Performance** (MCellUpdates/s, this 128-core/256-thread box):\n\n| case | original serial | 1 thread | 64 thr | 128 thr | 256 thr |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 640³, 20 iters | 710 | 737 | 16030 | 15932 | 15644 |\n| 128³, 100 iters | ~200 | 233 | 7144 | 10002 | 6062 |\n\n≈22x on the large grid (~250 GB/s, roughly memory-bandwidth bound). The 128³ case peaks at one thread per core; SMT oversubscription costs some throughput there, but I left thread count to the environment rather than overriding it."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":330,"cache_read_input_tokens":90744,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":330},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2e189a1e-32bb-4f46-b1cc-4629e7845138","uuid":"89fb4c0b-8082-4e02-9bd9-4117e93bbfce","timestamp":"2026-09-05T11:43:47.114Z","request_id":"req_011CekEVvQ4uGfRzuyvxN7DJ"}
